# Unpaved road statistics

## Overview

Unpaved share per class (with or without the Off-road mark), as **all km / tagged km only**, sorted by tertiary. The first number counts untagged roads as paved, so it is a minimum; the second divides by tagged km only, an upper bound because mappers tag surface more often when a road is not paved. Off-road mark and Untagged: share of all road km.

## Official figures

CIA World Factbook, last snapshot with road data (2025-04-10, [stats/official/factbook-roadways.json](official/factbook-roadways.json)); survey years vary from country to country. OSM: all seven road classes (no tracks, no service roads), all km / tagged km only. Definitions differ (Factbook "unpaved" usually includes tracks and rural roads that OSM may map as `highway=track`), so compare orders of magnitude, not exact values.

## Albania (al), data 2026-11-11

Run 2026-11-12, classifier `0ee7ee3811bf`, pipeline `77d37c8f435b`, osmium version 1.19.1, extract `albania-latest.osm.pbf`, data date from header.

| Class | km | Paved (tagged) | Untagged | Unpaved | of which Off-road mark |
|---|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|
| motorway | 403 | 100.0% | 0.0% | 0.0% | 0.0% |
| trunk | 445 | 99.3% | 0.7% | 0.0% | 0.0% |
| primary | 1,500 | 95.0% | 4.9% | 0.0% | 0.0% |
| secondary | 2,382 | 89.9% | 7.4% | 2.7% | 0.3% |
| tertiary | 3,741 | 53.9% | 20.8% | 25.3% | 6.2% |
| unclassified | 8,852 | 8.4% | 40.1% | 51.5% | 13.3% |
| residential | 14,729 | 12.9% | 64.7% | 22.3% | 1.5% |
| **All** | 32,051 | 28.3% | 44.1% | 27.6% | 5.1% |

Untagged: no `surface` and no known `tracktype`, so the road is assumed paved. Paved (tagged) + Untagged + Unpaved = 100%. The Off-road mark is the part of Unpaved with off-road evidence (`smoothness`, `tracktype`, `4wd_only`, or `surface=mud` or `rock`).
